As we employ a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning rate schedule, we further investigate the gains from learning rate annealing over a broad range of hyperparameters settings, models and data budgets, and whether the optimal learning rate and batch size \textit{transfer} between the stable and decay phases. Finally, we characterize the dependence of loss on model capacity and dataset size, evaluating recently proposed scaling forms that explicitly model their interaction. We find these approaches particularly effective at capturing both undertraining and overtraining regimes across our experiments. This study establishes a first baseline and scaling procedure for the development of future OpenEuroLLM models. We open-source the complete collection of pretraining runs used in this study.

</details>


### [285] [SymboLLM-FE: LLM-Accelerated Symbolic Regression for Automated Feature Engineering on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28408)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Zi-Yi Jia,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymboLLM-FE，结合符号回归与大语言模型（LLM），在仅需极少LLM调用的情况下，自动生成高性能且可解释的表格数据特征，解决了传统AutoFE可解释性差及现有LLM方法迭代成本高、存在幻觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据特征信息量不足限制模型性能；传统自动特征工程（AutoFE）生成的特征缺乏可解释性；基于LLM的AutoFE面临多轮迭代成本高、偏差及幻觉风险等挑战。

Method: 提出SymboLLM-FE框架：首先利用符号回归提取与目标变量强相关的数学表达式以保证性能，随后利用具备丰富先验知识的LLM对这些表达式进行精炼，通过基于统计先验的机制确保特征的可解释性，并将LLM调用次数控制在个位数。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集和四个Kaggle竞赛中的实证结果表明，SymboLLM-FE在模型性能上优于现有的AutoFE方法，同时显著减少了迭代次数并提升了特征的可解释性。

Conclusion: SymboLLM-FE成功解决了自动特征工程中性能、可解释性与计算效率之间的权衡问题，通过统计先验引导的LLM精炼机制和极低的LLM调用成本，实现了高效、可靠且可解释的特征生成。

Abstract: Tabular data, as a core data format in machine learning, often lacks the discriminative power needed for high-performance modeling due to insufficient feature informativeness. Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) overcomes this by automating feature generation and selection, ensuring both model performance and operational efficiency. However, traditional AutoFE often yield features with poor interpretability because they rely on blind mathematical transformations, while large language models (LLM)-based AutoFE faces challenges in requiring costly multi-round iterations to generate high-utility features to effectively enhance model performance, compounded by inherent risks of bias and hallucination. In this paper, we combine symbolic regression with LLMs for feature engineering (SymboLLM-FE) to solve these challenges. We extract mathematically expressive formulas strongly correlated with the target via symbolic regression, which can enhance model performance, then refine them by LLMs with rich prior knowledge to ensure interpretability. Empirical results on six real-world datasets and four Kaggle competitions demonstrate that SymboLLM-FE outperforms existing AutoFE. SymboLLM-FE also addresses the dual challenges of poor interpretability and numerous iterations by employing a statistical prior-grounded LLM refinement mechanism and single-digit LLM calls.

</details>


### [286] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），解决了傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在不同周期性域上无法迁移的问题，通过参数化连续物理波矢量谱核实现跨域一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 虽然与网格分辨率无关，但其离散谱权重依赖于周期性域的物理波矢量，导致在不同域上应用时代表不同算子，无法实现跨域迁移。

Method: 提出 EFNO，将谱核参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，使其能够学习在不同形状和大小的周期性域上一致作用的算子。

Result: 在热方程和材料科学中的交换关联势学习任务上验证，EFNO 能够泛化到未见过的网格大小和域。

Conclusion: EFNO 是 FNO 的域无关替代方案，适用于需要跨域迁移的任务，特别是在材料科学等领域具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>


### [287] [Curvature-Conditioned Multiscale Momentum with Sphere Constraints for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28442)
*Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Mingze Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种曲率条件多尺度动量方法，通过结合慢衰减和快衰减组件优化平坦方向的训练动态，并采用球面约束防止参数膨胀，显著加速了LLM预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练计算成本高，噪声主导梯度和病态损失景观带来挑战。现有自适应优化器（如AdamW、Muon）依赖梯度归一化，对病态曲率缓解有限，沿平坦方向的进展缓慢，影响最终损失降低。

Method: 提出曲率条件多尺度动量方法，仅在平坦方向应用：结合慢衰减组件降噪和快衰减组件快速适应曲率；采用球面约束技术防止参数膨胀和有效学习率过快衰减。

Result: 在多种架构（dense、MoE）和模型规模（0.12B-2.3B参数）上，该方法显著加速了Muon优化器；理论验证了加速效果并阐明了平坦方向多尺度动量的设计原理。

Conclusion: 该方法通过优化平坦方向的训练动态，有效解决了LLM预训练中的病态曲率问题，实现了稳定加速，为大规模模型训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pretraining accounts for a large fraction of the total computational cost in LLM training. However, noise-dominant gradients and the highly ill-conditioned loss landscape bring severe challenges. Although modern adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Muon have achieved great success in large-scale pretraining, their reliance on gradient normalization offers limited mitigation of the ill-conditioned curvature. The progress along flat directions (eigen-directions of small eigenvalues), which dominates the final loss reduction, remains relatively slow. To enhance training dynamics along flat directions, we propose a curvature-conditioned multiscale momentum method with sphere constraints, delivering steady acceleration in LLM pretraining. This multiscale momentum, applied only along flat directions, pairs a slow-decay component for noise reduction with a fast-decay component for rapid curvature adaptation, harnessing their complementary strengths. Crucially, we employ a sphere constraint technique to prevent parameter inflation and excessively rapid effective learning rate decay that would otherwise arise from a naive combination. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method significantly accelerates Muon across diverse architectures (dense, MoE) and model sizes (0.12B--2.3B parameters). Theoretically, we verify the acceleration effect and provide insight into the design principles underlying the flat-direction multiscale momentum.

</details>


### [288] [How Proper Scoring Rules Shape LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28482)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim,Ville A. Satopää,Philip E. Tetlock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了不同奖励函数（严格评分规则）对大语言模型（LLM）二元预测性能及行为的影响，发现尽管理论激励一致，但不同规则导致模型在校准度、概率使用及误差结构（偏差、信息、噪声）上存在显著差异，表明奖励函数的选择不仅影响预测精度，更决定了误差的构成方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在LLM作为预测器的场景中，选择不同的严格评分规则（proper scoring rules）作为训练目标，是否会导致模型表现出不同的性能特征和行为模式，尽管这些规则在理论上均激励真实的概率报告。

Method: 选取五种严格评分规则作为训练目标，对LLM进行二元真实世界事件预测的训练与评估；通过对比各模型在校准度、概率分布、以及分解后的偏差（bias）、信息（information）和噪声（noise）剖面，分析不同奖励函数下的模型表现差异。

Result: 不同评分规则训练出的模型在校准度和概率使用上差异显著，而在总体准确率和区分度上差异较小。具体而言，Brier评分训练的模型具有最低的观测Brier分数和最高的AUC-ROC，而Log评分训练的模型拥有最高的观测Log分数和最低的校准误差。性能相似的模型通过不同的偏差、信息和噪声组合达到了相同的聚合性能。

Conclusion: 严格评分规则作为训练目标时并非完全可互换；奖励函数的选择不仅塑造了LLM的预测能力，更决定了其预测误差的结构化特征（即偏差、信息与噪声的分布），尽管部分差异可能源于训练随机性（单种子实验）。

Abstract: This paper evaluates how reward function choice shapes the performance and behavior of LLM forecasters. We compare five proper scoring rules as training objectives for binary forecasts of resolved real-world events. Although the rules share the same theoretical incentive for truthful probability reporting, the resulting models differ in calibration, probability use, and estimated profiles of bias, information, and noise, with smaller differences in aggregate accuracy and discrimination. The Brier-trained model has the lowest observed Brier score and highest AUC-ROC, while the log-trained model has the highest observed log score and lowest calibration error. Models with similar aggregate performance also reach that performance through different combinations of bias, information, and noise. Proper scoring rules therefore need not behave interchangeably as training objectives. Reward choice may shape not only how well an LLM forecasts, but how its forecasting errors are structured. Each condition uses a single seed, so some differences may reflect training stochasticity.

</details>


### [289] [REPLICANT: Learning Policies for Evading and Hardening Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28499)
*Shae McFadden,Ilias Tsingenopoulos,Mario D'Onghia,Alexander Herzog,Myles Foley,Chris Hicks,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Fabio Pierazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Replicant，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，在严格的标签黑盒威胁模型下学习恶意软件 evasion 策略。该方法在攻击成功率和查询效率上均优于现有技术，并能通过对抗训练显著提升检测器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习恶意软件检测评估往往基于不现实的攻击假设（如访问训练数据、特征空间或置信度分数），无法有效模拟真实世界中能力强大的对手。因此，需要在更严格的标签黑盒模型下评估检测系统的真实鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Replicant框架，利用深度强化学习（Deep Reinforcement Learning）学习可复用的策略，决定如何修改恶意软件样本以及何时向目标检测器发起查询。该策略能够在不同样本、检测器和特征空间之间进行迁移。

Result: 在7个Android恶意软件检测器和3种特征空间的测试中，Replicant实现了78.8%的平均攻击成功率，相比最先进方法提升了20.9%-39.2%，且具有最高的查询效率。此外，将其用于对抗训练时，能生成具有更强泛化鲁棒性的检测器。

Conclusion: 学习 evasion 任务不仅能产生更强的攻击性能，更重要的是为加固恶意软件检测器提供了更优质的信号，证明了在严格黑盒模型下进行对抗评估和训练的有效性。

Abstract: To determine the real-world effectiveness of machine learning based malware detection, it is vital to evaluate its robustness against highly capable adversaries. However, state-of-the-art attacks do not effectively model realistic adversaries, as they often assume access to privileged information such as the training data, feature space, or confidence scores of the target. In this work, we present Replicant, a deep reinforcement learning framework that learns the realistic task of evasion under a strict label-only black-box threat model. Replicant learns a reusable policy on how to modify a malware sample and when to query the target, which transfers across samples, detectors, and feature spaces. Across seven Android malware detectors and three feature spaces, Replicant is the strongest and most query-efficient approach achieving a mean attack success rate of 78.8%, a relative improvement of 20.9%-39.2% over the state-of-the-art. Furthermore, when used for adversarial training, Replicant also outperforms the state-of-the art by producing detectors with more generalizable robustness. With Replicant we demonstrate that learning the task of evasion not only results in stronger attack performance but, crucially, provides a better signal for hardening malware detectors.

</details>


### [290] [An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28541)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了采样门（sampling gate）认证下的代码世界模型在不可达区域的拓扑错误问题。研究发现，模型在可达区域内精确，但在被“环形冻结”包围的不可达区域内可能存在任意拓扑错误且无法被外部检测。通过LLM合成实验，文章揭示了通道宽度（gamma）如何影响错误的可证伪性与危害性，并提出了基于拓扑维度匹配的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明在存在观测盲区（如环形冻结模式）时，经认证的世界模型其认知边界、潜在错误成本及拓扑错误的特性，特别是解决“模型在可见范围内完美但在不可见范围内完全错误”这一根本性不确定性问题。

Method: 构建了最小环仪器（minimal ring instrument）理论模型，定义“门商”（gate quotient）以量化可达性；利用三个模型家族的LLM合成技术，通过调节通道宽度（gamma）参数，实证分析拓扑伪影（wrong-topology filled-disc artifact）在三种机制（不可证伪且无害、可证伪且昂贵、瞬间被证伪）间的转变；应用持续同调（persistent homology）分析拓扑特征。

Result: 1. 危险性取决于相对于可达性的拓扑：规划者可用的通道能消除盲目模型的利用（成本从1.09降至~0），而隐藏通道即使贝蒂数相同仍保持高危害（1.12）。2. 修复受限于参数和传感器：外部证据无法恢复区域，内部模型虽能构建正确拓扑但无法确定参数，且受传感器几何分辨率限制。3. 缓解需匹配错误的维度与方向：点围栏无效，维度匹配的持续围栏可将利用成本从0.999降至0.058，对偶自由证书可将发明模式失败从1.769降至0.029。

Conclusion: 在n维空间中，壳层（shell）导致的误识别几乎是必然的，但其危险性依然完全可被利用；误识别的概率与潜在危险的两个轴线是相互独立的。因此，认证模型的安全性不仅取决于观测覆盖，更取决于对不可达区域拓扑错误的维度匹配缓解策略。

Abstract: A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. Third, mitigation must match the error's dimension and direction: point fences fail against the one-dimensional boundary, a dimension-matched persisted fence collapses exploitation to a two-lesson transient (0.999 to 0.058), and the dual freedom certificate collapses the invented-mode failure symmetrically (1.769 to 0.029). In n dimensions the shell makes misidentification near-certain while the danger stays fully exploitable: the two axes are independent.

</details>


### [291] [DARTS: Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28547)
*Aaryan Ajay Sharma,Sai Nishanth Padala,Seganrasan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对解码器大模型合并中的表示偏差问题，提出了DARTS方法。该方法通过引入熵加权L1损失和逐位置加法偏差，解决了因果注意力导致的偏差累积及不同位置重要性不均的问题，在代码生成、数学推理和指令遵循任务上显著提升了合并模型性能，且仅增加极少量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并技术会导致合并模型的隐藏状态与源模型之间产生系统性偏差（表示偏差）。虽然已有研究针对编码器模型提出了修正方案，但由于解码器模型的自回归特性，其表示偏差问题尚未被深入研究，且面临因果注意力掩码导致偏差累积以及不同token位置重要性不均两大独特挑战。

Method: 提出Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS) 方法。该方法包含两个核心创新：1) 采用新颖的熵加权L1损失函数，对高熵（决策关键）位置的修正赋予更高权重；2) 引入逐位置加法偏差机制，以捕捉位置依赖性误差，同时避免过度参数化。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B模型上，针对代码生成 (HumanEval)、数学推理 (GSM8K) 和指令遵循 (AlpacaEval) 三个领域的广泛评估显示，DARTS相比标准微调方法取得了显著性能提升，且仅增加了总参数量的0.1%。

Conclusion: DARTS有效解决了解码器模型合并中的表示偏差问题，通过针对性的位置依赖修正和熵加权策略，在几乎不增加参数开销的前提下，显著改善了多任务合并模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific fine-tuned LLMs into a single multi-task model without additional training. However, merged models are known to suffer from representation bias: systematic drift between the merged model's hidden states and those of each individual source model. Prior work (Yang et al., 2024a) study and mitigate this bias for encoder-based vision models using a lightweight correction module trained with L1 loss. However, such bias is not studied for decoder models due to their autoregressive nature. We analyze the problem of representation bias in decoder models, and show two challenges absent in encoders: (1) the causal attention mask causes bias to accumulate across token positions, requiring position-dependent correction; and (2) not all token positions are equally important, i.e., high-entropy (decision-critical) positions matter far more than low-entropy ones. To address these challenges, we propose Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS). DARTS employs a novel entropy-weighted L1 loss to upweight correction at high-entropy positions where errors most affect generation quality, and a per-position additive bias that captures position-dependent error without overparameterization. We perform extensive evaluation on three domains: code generation (HumanEval), mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), and instruction following (AlpacaEval) on Llama-2-7B models, and show DARTS achieves significant improvement over the standard surgery approach while adding negligible parameters ($0.1\%$ of total parameters).

</details>


### [292] [Advancing Interaction-Sensitive Feature Selection: Novel Relief-Based Algorithms, Expanded Comparisons, and Recommendations for Biomedical Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28552)
*Kia Kazemi-Nia,Harsh Bandhey,Philip J. Freda,Ryan J. Urbanowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究重构并优化了scikit-rebate Python包，引入了多种新的Relief类算法（RBA）变体，并通过基因组模拟数据进行了严格基准测试。结果显示，新算法（特别是MultiSWRFDB*）在检测特征交互作用方面表现优异，且重构后的代码运行速度提升了10至35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维生物医学数据建模中特征选择的难题：现有的过滤法难以检测特征交互作用，而包装法或嵌入法计算成本过高。Relief类算法（RBA）虽能兼顾交互作用检测与计算效率，但仍需进一步优化和扩展以提升性能。

Method: 1. 重构、优化并扩展scikit-rebate包，纳入SWRF*、mu-Relief及5种新提出的RBA变体（采用不同的邻居选择和特征评分策略）。2. 在不同样本量、特征数、遗传力及关联类型（主效应与交互作用）的模拟基因组数据集上，对所有RBA进行预测特征排序能力和运行时间的基准比较。

Result: 1. 除mu-Relief外，所有RBA均能有效检测噪声数据中的2-way交互作用。2. 采用"far"评分策略的RBA在检测2-way交互作用上表现最佳（MultiSWRFDB*最优），但对主效应敏感度较低。3. SWRF、MultiSWRF、MultiSURF和MultiSWRFDB在主效应和2-way交互作用数据集中综合表现最佳，其中MultiSWRFDB在涉及3-way交互作用时表现最优。4. 代码重构使RBA运行时间减少了10至35倍。

Conclusion: 新引入的RBA变体性能强劲，能够稳健地保留主效应和2-way上位性交互作用，从而为下游建模保存了关键的预测信号；同时，scikit-rebate的重构显著降低了计算成本，使其更适用于高维生物医学数据分析。

Abstract: As a precursor to high-dimensional biomedical data modeling, reliable feature selection can reduce computational expense, improve modeling performance, and yield simpler, more interpretable models. However, most filter-based feature selection methods struggle to detect feature interactions, while wrapper or embedded feature selection methods are computationally expensive. Relief-based algorithms (RBAs) are filter methods that are sensitive to feature interactions while mitigating these other limitations. This study (1) refactors, optimizes, and expands the scikit-rebate Python package with existing and newly proposed RBA variants and (2) conducts rigorous RBA benchmark comparisons across diverse genomic simulations. We expand scikit-rebate to include SWRF*, mu-Relief, and 5 novel RBA variants implementing alternative strategies for neighbor selection and feature scoring. All RBAs were evaluated to compare predictive feature ranking and runtime across simulated genomic datasets varying in sample size, number of features, heritability, and underlying association type (e.g. main effects and interactions). All RBAs, except mu-Relief, were proficient in detecting 2-way interactions in noisy data. RBAs utilizing 'far' scoring were best at detecting 2-way interactions - with MultiSWRFDB* top-performing - but were far less sensitive to main effects. SWRF, MultiSWRF, MultiSURF, and MultiSWRFDB yielded top performance across main effect and 2-way interaction datasets with MultiSWRFDB performing best when also considering 3-way interactions. Refactoring of scikit-rebate resulted in 10 to 35-fold reductions in RBA runtimes. The newly introduced RBAs were among the strongest performing, and by robustly retaining both main effects and 2-way epistatic interactions, these algorithms preserve predictive signals for downstream modeling.

</details>


### [293] [Blog: Survey of Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28557)
*Ruoran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2025-2026 年神经网络优化不再仅是 Adam 变体的迭代，设计空间已扩展至矩阵、层、时间策略及系统表示。本文从时间估计、更新几何、范围管理和表示系统四个维度梳理优化器，指出矩阵感知方法虽有进步，但无普适替代 AdamW 的方案，需根据具体场景选择优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于 Adam 变体的改进，但神经网络优化设计空间已显著扩展，需系统性梳理新兴优化方法及其适用场景，以指导实际应用中优化器的选择与设计。

Method: 沿四个独立轴（时间估计、更新几何、范围管理、表示与系统）对近期优化器和训练优化方法进行组织与分类，连接 Muon 谱归一化、Shampoo/SOAP 历史矩阵统计、自适应混合矩阵方法、内存高效优化器、无调度训练、小批量校正及量化优化器状态等技术。

Result: 矩阵感知方法代表真实进步，但不存在与上下文无关的 AdamW 替代方案；优化器排名随模型规模、数据参数比、批量大小、调度、参数分区、调优预算及目标指标（tokens/FLOPs/时间/内存）而变化。

Conclusion: 优化器设计应采用组合视角，并建立更严格的评估协议；实际应用中需根据具体场景（模型规模、资源约束、目标指标等）选择合适优化器，而非追求单一最优解。

Abstract: Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

</details>


### [294] [QGPINNs: A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Nonlocal Differential Equations on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28589)
*Vaibhav Mehandiratta,Saket Ramchandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QGPINNs，一个基于PyTorch的物理信息神经网络框架，用于求解量子图上的非局部微分方程。该框架通过图基损失函数耦合各边的神经网络近似，并整合了多种学习策略以提高精度和稳定性，适用于正向和反向问题，并在多个实际网络中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子图上非局部微分方程的数值求解难题，现有方法在处理复杂图结构、顶点耦合条件及弱奇异性解时存在局限性，需要一种灵活、高效且物理一致的通用计算框架。

Method: 开发QGPINNs框架，使用神经网络近似每条边上的解，通过统一图基损失函数强制执行控制方程及初始、边界和顶点传输条件（包括连续性、Kirchhoff-Neumann条件和Dirichlet条件）。整合软/硬约束、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入和可学习奇异性捕捉特征等图适应学习策略，并扩展至反问题求解。

Result: 在基准图结构和真实网络（如IEEE 14总线系统和农田排水网络）上的数值实验验证了该框架的准确性、计算效率和物理一致性，能够有效识别分数阶算子阶数和物理参数。

Conclusion: QGPINNs为量子图上非局部微分方程提供了通用、高效且物理一致的求解框架，通过图适应学习策略显著提升了精度和训练稳定性，适用于正向和反向问题，具有广泛的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose QGPINNs, a physics-informed neural network framework developed in PyTorch for the numerical solution of nonlocal differential equations on quantum graphs. The framework is designed as a general computational implementation in which the solution on each edge of the graph is approximated by a neural network, while a unified graph-based loss function enforces the governing equations together with initial, boundary, and vertex transmission conditions. In particular, the formulation incorporates standard continuity and Kirchhoff-Neumann vertex conditions and Dirichlet boundary conditions into the learning process to couple the local edge-wise neural approximations into a global solution on the graph. The framework is developed for two representative classes of nonlinear models: multi-order fractional elliptic problems and time-fractional evolution equations on quantum graphs. To improve accuracy and training stability, QGPINNs integrates several graph-adapted learning strategies, including soft and hard constraint enforcement, dynamic loss balancing, Fourier feature embeddings, and a learnable singularity-capturing feature for weakly singular solutions arising in the considered problems. The framework also extends naturally to inverse problems, including the identification of the orders of fractional operators and physical parameters from noisy observational data. We validate the accuracy, computational efficiency, and physical consistency of the proposed framework through numerical experiments on benchmark graph structures and real-world networks, including the IEEE 14-bus system and an open-channel agricultural drainage network.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [Improved Subexponential Upper Bounds for $3$-Restricted Matching Vector Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27859)
*Sidhant Saraogi*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定条件下，3-受限匹配向量族（MVF）的大小上界为 $2^{O(\sqrt{n\log n \log m})}$，显著改进了之前的 $2^{O(n/\log n)}$ 上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配向量族（MVF）是构建最佳常数查询局部可解码码（LDC）的关键工具，但对其大小的精确上界理解有限，尤其是对于受限 MVF。

Method: 采用新的多项式方法论证，通过控制匹配向量在和集中的碰撞来推导上界。

Result: 在 $m \leq \sqrt{n}$ 的条件下，$\mathbb{Z}_m^n$ 中 3-受限 MVF 的大小上界从 $2^{O(n/\log n)}$ 提升至 $2^{O(\sqrt{n\log n \log m})}$。

Conclusion: 该结果显著改进了 Bhowmick 等人（STOC'13, SICOMP'14）的先前最佳上界，为理解受限 MVF 的结构和 LDC 的构造提供了更紧的理论界限。

Abstract: Matching Vector families (MVFs) are defined by two ordered lists of vectors in $\mathbb{Z}_m^n$ whose inner products satisfy specific residue patterns modulo an integer $m$. Most famously, restricted MVFs are used to construct the best-known constant-query Locally Decodable codes (LDCs).
  We prove an upper bound of $2^{O\left(\sqrt{n\log n \log m}\right)}$ on the size of $3$-restricted MVFs in $\mathbb{Z}_m^n$ for $m \leq \sqrt{n}$, substantially improving on the previous best bound of $2^{O(n/\log n)}$ by Bhowmick, Dvir and Lovett (STOC'13, SICOMP'14). Our proof relies on a new polynomial method argument that controls collisions in sumsets of matching vectors.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Optimal Transport for Network Comparison: A Review with Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27500)
*James Hyun,François G. Meyer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用最优传输比较无向无权图的三种主要距离（Wasserstein、Gromov-Wasserstein 和 Bures-Wasserstein），并通过合成数据集聚类与真实时间序列网络异常检测进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图度量无法解释图之间的变换过程，而最优传输不仅能计算网络不相似度，还能提供描述图如何相互转换的传输计划，因此需要系统综述其在网络比较中的应用。

Method: 1. 通过节点特征概率分布分析一维情形下 Wasserstein 距离的闭式解；2. 展示 Wasserstein 与 Gromov-Wasserstein 距离的传输计划如何捕捉图扰动后影响距离的具体节点；3. 利用拉普拉斯谱推导 Bures-Wasserstein 距离的边界以避免完整谱分解；4. 使用合成网络数据集进行聚类评估，并用真实世界时间序列网络进行异常检测。

Result: 成功推导了 Bures-Wasserstein 距离的谱边界，验证了传输计划在识别关键节点方面的有效性，并在聚类与异常检测任务中展示了三种距离的实际性能。

Conclusion: 最优传输距离为网络比较提供了可解释的度量工具，尤其在捕捉节点级影响与避免高计算成本方面具有优势，适用于聚类与异常检测等任务。

Abstract: Network comparison using optimal transport is a growing area of research in network science. Unlike standard graph metrics, optimal transport computes both network dissimilarity and a transport plan that explains how one graph morphs into another. In this paper, we review how optimal transport compares undirected, unweighted graphs using three primary distances: the Wasserstein, Gromov-Wasserstein, and Bures-Wasserstein distances. We examine the closed form of the Wasserstein distance in one dimension via node feature probability distributions, and show how the transport plans of the Wasserstein and Gromov-Wasserstein distances capture which specific nodes influence the distance after graph perturbation. For the Bures-Wasserstein distance, we derive bounds using Laplacian spectra to bypass full spectral decompositions. Finally, we evaluate these distances using a synthetic network dataset for clustering and a real-world time series network for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [297] [Towards a mathematical theory of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27540)
*Michael I. Ivanitskiy,John Jasper,Emily J. King,Dustin G. Mixon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用框架理论和压缩感知工具，建立了神经网络叠加的数学理论，证明了在随机和最坏情况支持集下，通过ReLU激活和特定偏置向量可实现稀疏特征的高概率恢复，并确定了实等角紧框架下的精确恢复阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决神经网络中特征叠加（superposition）现象的数学建模问题，利用框架理论和压缩感知工具来理解和保证在过完备字典下的稀疏特征恢复能力。

Method: 构建了一个数学模型，其中稀疏二进制特征向量通过过完备字典编码，并通过应用带有适当偏置向量的ReLU激活函数（$\operatorname{ReLU}(W^\top W x+b)$）进行特征恢复。研究采用了随机支持集和最坏情况支持集两种设定，结合高斯随机矩阵和等角紧框架进行分析，并提出了关于Gram矩阵符号分布的新表征。

Result: 在随机支持集设定下，证明了对于近乎紧且低相干性的字典，当期望稀疏度高达 $d/\log n$ 量级时，可实现高概率的支持集恢复；在最坏情况设定下，给出了允许支持集恢复的尖锐且可计算的稀疏度准则，并确定了实等角紧框架（$n>d+1$）基于相干性的精确恢复阈值。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅为神经网络中的叠加现象提供了坚实的理论保证和恢复定理，还通过揭示Gram矩阵符号分布的新特性，为框架理论领域提供了具有独立价值的数学成果。

Abstract: We develop a mathematical theory of superposition in neural networks using tools from frame theory and compressed sensing. In our model, a sparse binary vector \(x\) of active features is encoded through an overcomplete dictionary \(W\), and feature recovery is performed by applying \(\operatorname{ReLU}(W^\top W x+b)\) with an appropriate bias vector \(b\). We prove several recovery theorems for this model. In the random-support setting, we establish high-probability support recovery for nearly tight, low-coherence dictionaries, with guarantees when the expected sparsity is up to order \(d/\log n\). In the worst-case support setting, we give a sharp and computable criterion for which sparsity levels permit support recovery. We apply this criterion to Gaussian random matrices and equiangular tight frames. For real equiangular tight frames with \(n>d+1\), we determine the exact recovery threshold in terms of the coherence. The proof of this result for real equiangular tight frames relies on a novel characterization---which should be of independent interest to frame theorists---of the distribution of signs in the Gram matrix.

</details>


### [298] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28179)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究风险规避决策，通过优化确定性等价（OCE）指标衡量风险，提出已知分布下的最优策略及未知分布下的数据驱动校准方法，并在无线波束成形场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决代理在系统状态不确定情况下的风险规避决策问题，需要一种能泛化均值 - 方差风险和条件风险价值（CVaR）等流行标准的风险度量方法，并为共形预测类型的预测集提供操作解释。

Method: 1. 使用优化确定性等价（OCE）指标衡量风险；2. 刻画已知分布下的最优策略，证明其可简化为基于预测集的 CVaR 解决方案；3. 针对未知分布，开发基于似然合成模型和保留校准数据的数据驱动校准策略。

Result: 在已知分布下明确了最优策略并建立与预测集的联系；在未知分布下实现了 OCE 风险的高概率控制；在两个无线波束成形设置中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的 OCE 风险度量框架及数据驱动校准策略能有效处理不确定状态下的风险规避决策，为共形预测提供新解释，并在实际无线通信场景中展现应用价值。

Abstract: We study risk-averse decision making, in which an agent selects actions while being uncertain about the true system state. The risk is measured via optimized certainty equivalent (OCE) metrics, which generalize popular criteria such as mean-variance risk and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR). We characterize the optimal policy under known distributions, and show that it reduces to a prediction set-based solution for the CVaR. This provides an operational interpretation of conformal prediction-type prediction sets. For unknown distributions, we develop a data-driven calibration strategy, based on a synthetic model for the likelihood and held-out calibration data, yielding high-probability control of the OCE risk. The approach is evaluated on two wireless beamforming settings.

</details>


### [299] [I-FLOP: Fast Learning of Order and Parents from Interventional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28245)
*Liuting Chen,Alex Markham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将FLOP算法从观测数据扩展到干预数据，提出I-FLOP，在性能和运行时间上优于现有因果结构学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FLOP算法仅适用于观测数据，需要扩展到干预数据以利用干预信息提高因果结构学习的准确性。

Method: 采用Hauser和Bühlmann(2012)的干预BIC评分，并将其适配到基于迭代Cholesky的评分更新机制中，以保持FLOP的速度优势。

Result: 在样本极限下，I-FLOP能恢复与数据生成DAG相同的干预马尔可夫等价类中的DAG；在真实和模拟干预数据上，性能和运行时间均优于现有算法。

Conclusion: I-FLOP成功将FLOP扩展到干预数据，在保持高效的同时提升了因果结构学习能力。

Abstract: We extend the FLOP (fast learning of order and parents) algorithm recently proposed by Wienöbst et al. (2026) from observational to interventional data. In particular, we use the interventional BIC score of Hauser and Bühlmann (2012), adapting it to be used with the iterative Cholesky-based score updates that are partly responsible for FLOP's speed. We show that, in the sample limit, I-FLOP recovers a DAG in the same interventional Markov equivalence class as the data-generating DAG. We compare I-FLOP to existing causal structure learning algorithms on real and simulated interventional data, where it performs favorably in terms of both performance and run time.

</details>


### [300] [Localizing Global Discrepancies: Marginal Contributions and Contextual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28375)
*Tommaso dorigo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将全局拟合优度统计量局部化到单个观测值的框架，通过计算观测值在随机统计上下文中的条件或边缘贡献，连接了重采样诊断、数据估值与异常检测，并导出了更高效的估计器，在LHC异常检测基准上表现优异，同时揭示了上下文在共享潜在结构模型中提供额外判别信息的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局拟合优度和差异统计量虽能判定样本偏离参考分布，但无法识别导致偏离的具体观测值（即局部化问题），本文旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 构建一个框架，为每个观测值分配其在随机统计上下文中的条件或边缘贡献；利用对称统计量的固定大小替换等价于中心条件局部化，U统计量的加法评分对应一阶Hoeffding/Hájek贡献，平滑分布泛函关联影响函数，无偏已知背景MMD归约为MMD见证；提出匹配上下文减法以消除无关波动，并设计成对MMD事件包含项作为简单局部化器。

Result: 在LHC Olympics异常检测基准上，成对估计器以预测的1/(Rm^2)速率收敛至直接经验MMD见证，在m=1000、R=5x10^6时达到0.9993的相关系数且AUC几乎相同；在共享潜在变量的玩具模型中，孤立事件AUC为0.5，但集成方法利用跨事件依赖性恢复了判别信息，而独立潜在变量控制组则不能。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅实现了全局差异的高效局部化，还证明了当备择假设包含共享结构时，上下文能提供超越事件自身特征的额外类别信息，从而区分了上下文的两种角色：高效局部化与提供额外判别信息。

Abstract: Global goodness-of-fit and discrepancy statistics can establish that a sample departs from a reference distribution without identifying which observations drive the departure. We develop a framework for this localization problem by assigning to each observation its conditional or marginal contribution across random statistical contexts. This connects resampling diagnostics and data valuation to projection theory and event-level anomaly detection. For symmetric statistics, fixed-size replacement is exactly equivalent to centered conditional localization. For U-statistics, the addition score equals the first Hoeffding/Hájek contribution; for smooth distributional functionals it is related at leading order to the influence function; and for unbiased known-background MMD it reduces exactly to the MMD witness.
  This viewpoint also yields more efficient estimators. Matched-context subtraction removes fluctuations unrelated to the observation, while for pairwise MMD the event-containing terms give a simple localizer. On the LHC Olympics anomaly-detection benchmark, the pair estimator converges to the direct empirical MMD witness with the predicted 1/(Rm^2) scaling, where m is batch size and R the number of batches. At m=1000 and R=5x106 it reaches correlation 0.9993 with essentially identical AUC.
  We also ask when context contains information beyond an event's own features. In a shared-latent toy model, the full single-event signal and background distributions are identical by construction, forcing isolated-event AUC=0.5. Discriminating information survives only in cross-event dependence induced by the shared latent parameter; the ensemble recovers this information, whereas an independent-latent control does not. This separates two roles of context: efficient localization of a global discrepancy and genuinely additional class information when the alternative contains shared structure.

</details>


### [301] [Learning between the peaks: sharp asymptotics for kernel ridge regression under power-law anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28564)
*Lorenzo Rizzi,Arie Wortsman Zurich,Bruno Loureiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性高斯数据下的核岭回归，推导了核谱和泛化误差的渐近精确表达式，揭示了各向异性如何重塑学习曲线，阐明了输入几何形状如何影响核特征及其泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解各向异性数据分布对核方法学习性能的影响，特别是在高维多项式制度下，各向异性如何改变核谱和泛化误差的行为。

Method: 在各向异性高斯数据假设下，对多项式内积核进行理论分析，推导核谱和泛化误差的渐近精确表达式，分析弱各向异性（0<α<1）和强各向异性（α>1）两种情况下的学习曲线特征。

Result: 弱各向异性时问题仍保持高维特性但方差峰值被抑制，目标与主方向强对齐时偏置在分数样本复杂度处下降；强各向异性时有效维度恒定，方差与样本量无关，偏置根据目标衰减速率呈现突变或幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 输入数据的几何形状（各向异性程度）从根本上塑造了核特征并显著影响泛化性能，各向异性参数α决定了学习曲线的形态和过渡行为。

Abstract: We study kernel ridge regression under anisotropic Gaussian data, where the input covariance decays as a power law with exponent $α\geq 0$ for polynomial inner-product kernels. We derive asymptotically sharp expressions for the kernel spectrum and the generalization error in the polynomial high-dimensional regime $n=Θ(d^κ)$, revealing how anisotropy reshapes the learning curves. For weak anisotropy ($0<α<1$), the problem remains effectively high-dimensional and retains some features of the isotropic case, while departing from it in others: the variance still peaks at integer sample complexities $κ\in\mathbb{N}$, but these peaks are progressively damped as $α$ grows; meanwhile, for targets strongly aligned with the data's principal directions, the bias drops at fractional sample complexities, decoupling the bias transitions from the interpolation peaks. For strong anisotropy ($α> 1$), the effective dimension of the problem is constant, and the variance stops depending on sample size altogether, plateauing under ridgeless interpolation or vanishing at an explicit rate under fixed ridge penalty. The bias undergoes a sharp transition governed by the target's decay rate: below a threshold, learning is abrupt rather than gradual; above it, the bias decays as a power law that recovers the classical source and capacity rates. We finally specialize these results to single-index targets, showing how the alignment of the index with the data's principal directions determines the effect of anisotropy on learning. Together, our results clarify how the input geometry shapes the kernel features and fundamentally impacts its generalization properties.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [302] [Fine-Grained Complexity of Approximating Vector Knapsack: A Faster Algorithm and Bicriteria Optimality in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27600)
*Karl Bringmann,Ariel Kulik,Karol Węgrzycki*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对$d$维向量背包问题（$d$-Knapsack）的折半搜索（meet-in-the-middle）算法，将运行时间指数从$\lceil d/\varepsilon\rceil-d$显著降低至约$\lceil\frac{d-1}{2\varepsilon}-\frac12\rceil$，实现了25年来的首次突破。同时，基于$k$-SUM假设建立了精细的下界，证明了2-Knapsack问题的指数最优性，并给出了双准则最优的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决$d$维向量背包问题在$d\ge2$时近似方案运行时间指数过高的问题。自2000年以来，该问题的最佳近似方案运行时间指数为$\lceil d/\varepsilon\rceil-d$，长期未有改进。研究旨在通过降低该指数因子，突破现有算法瓶颈，并确定该问题在精细复杂度理论下的最优指数。

Method: 设计了首个针对$d$-Knapsack的折半搜索算法。核心创新在于摒弃了先前算法中使用的线性规划（LP）求解器，转而基于LP的结构化论证，设计了一种高效的动态规划算法来生成代表性解（representative solutions），从而大幅缩减搜索空间并提升效率。

Result: 1. 算法改进：将运行时间优化为$\widetilde O_{d,\varepsilon,ρ}(n^{\lceil\frac{d-1}{2\varepsilon}-\frac12+ρ\rceil}+n^d)$，首次将指数降低了常数因子。
2. 下界证明：基于$k$-SUM假设，证明了2-Knapsack需要$n^{\lceil\frac1{2\varepsilon}-\frac12 \rceil-o(1)}$的时间，确立了其最优指数为$\frac1{2\varepsilon}\pm O(1)$。
3. 特例最优性：针对2-Knapsack实现了$(1-\varepsilon-δ)$-近似，运行时间为$\widetilde O_{δ,\varepsilon}(n^{\lceil\frac1{2\varepsilon}-\frac12\rceil})$，达到双准则最优（bicriteria-optimal）。

Conclusion: 该研究结束了$d$-Knapsack问题近似方案运行时间25年无进展的局面，首次将指数精度从因子$O(1)$提升至加法$O(1)$级别。通过结合折半搜索策略与新型动态规划技术，不仅提供了更快的算法，还从精细复杂度角度严格界定了2-Knapsack问题的计算难度下限，证明了所提算法在指数层面的最优性。

Abstract: We revisit the $d$-dimensional Vector Knapsack problem ($d$-Knapsack): Given a $d$-dimensional capacity vector and a set of items, each with a $d$-dimensional weight vector and a profit, the goal is to select a set of items that maximizes the total profit without exceeding the capacity in any dimension. For any $d\ge2$, the best known approximation scheme for $d$-Knapsack runs in time $O(n^{\lceil d/\varepsilon\rceil-d})$ [Caprara, Kellerer, Pferschy, Pisinger '00].
  We improve this running time to $\widetilde O_{d,\varepsilon,ρ}(n^{\lceil\frac{d-1}{2\varepsilon}-\frac12+ρ\rceil}+n^d)$ for any $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ and every parameter $ρ\in(0,1)$. We achieve this speedup by designing the first meet-in-the-middle algorithm for $d$-Knapsack. This requires replacing the LP solver used in prior algorithms by a highly efficient dynamic programming algorithm to generate representative solutions, building on an LP-based structural argument. This is the first improvement in over 25 years, and the first result that improves the exponent by a constant factor.
  We complement this by a fine-grained lower bound based on $k$-SUM showing that 2-Knapsack requires time $n^{\lceil\frac1{2\varepsilon}-\frac12 \rceil-o(1)}$. This establishes the optimal exponent of 2-Knapsack as $\frac1{2\varepsilon}\pm O(1)$, which is precise up to an additive $O(1)$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first result that determines the optimal exponent more precisely than up to a factor $O(1)$, for any problem that admits a PTAS but no EPTAS.
  For the special case of 2-Knapsack we further attain a $(1-\varepsilon-δ)$-approximation in time $\widetilde O_{δ,\varepsilon}(n^{\lceil\frac1{2\varepsilon}-\frac12\rceil})$. This nearly matches our lower bound, as for a slightly better approximation ratio a slightly better running time is impossible -- so our algorithm is bicriteria-optimal.

</details>


### [303] [A Note on Approximating the Rural Postman Problem below 3/2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27607)
*Hong Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对乡村邮递员问题的近似算法，其近似比严格小于3/2。该结果通过借鉴Karlin等人针对度量旅行商问题的最大熵分布采样技术实现。此外，文章还指出对于任意固定ε>0，度量旅行商问题的α-近似算法可导出乡村邮递员问题的(α+ε)-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乡村邮递员问题（Rural Postman Problem）是组合优化中的经典问题，旨在寻找覆盖指定边集的最短闭合路径。现有算法的近似比限制在3/2，本文旨在突破这一界限，通过引入旅行商问题（TSP）中的先进采样技术来改进近似性能。

Method: 采用从最大熵分布中采样的技术，该技术最初由Karlin、Klein和Oveis Gharan用于解决度量旅行商问题。作者将这一技术适配到乡村邮递员问题中，并利用Lampis关于旅行商问题不可近似性研究中对必须遍历边的处理方法，建立了TSP近似算法与乡村邮递员问题近似算法之间的联系。

Result: 成功设计出一个近似比严格小于3/2的乡村邮递员问题近似算法。同时证明了对于任意固定ε>0，任何度量旅行商问题的α-近似算法都可以转化为乡村邮递员问题的(α+ε)-近似算法。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅改进了乡村邮递员问题的近似比上限，还揭示了其与旅行商问题在近似算法设计上的深层联系，为后续相关组合优化问题的研究提供了新的技术路径和理论依据。

Abstract: We give an approximation algorithm for the rural postman problem with approximation ratio strictly smaller than $3/2$. We obtain this result by adapting to the rural postman problem the technique of sampling from maximum entropy distributions for the metric traveling salesman problem of Karlin, Klein, and Oveis Gharan. We also observe that, for every fixed $\varepsilon>0$, any $α$-approximation algorithm for the metric traveling salesman problem yields an $(α+\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for the rural postman problem; this implication is already implicit in the treatment of edges that must be traversed in the work of Lampis on the inapproximability of the traveling salesman problem.

</details>


### [304] [Diva++: Dynamic Range Filtering over Hard Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27616)
*Navid Eslami,Ioana O. Bercea,Niv Dayan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Diva和Diva++，这是首个同时解决无性能保证、不支持变长键/范围查询以及不支持动态更新这三大缺陷的范围过滤器（Range Filter）。Diva通过采样构建Trie并存储关键区分位（infix），Diva++进一步利用熵编码和二进制Trie优化内存，两者在真实数据集上实现了与现有最先进技术相当的误报率，同时支持动态性和变长操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的范围过滤器在近似范围空查询中存在三个主要缺陷：缺乏误报率或性能的理论保证、不支持变长键和查询范围、以及不支持动态更新操作。

Method: Diva通过采样键构建缓存高效的Trie来学习数据分布，通过移除最长公共前缀和截断后缀来压缩键，仅保留足以区分排序顺序的中间位（infix），并将其存储在支持分裂以处理插入的动态数据块中。Diva++作为增强版，利用保序熵编码消除infix冗余，去除重复infix，并将节省的空间用于在紧凑二进制Trie中存储更多原始键位。

Result: 数学证明表明Diva在多种常见真实数据分布上提供了内存与误报率之间的最佳权衡。实验对比显示，Diva和Diva++在真实数据集上的误报率与现有技术持平，同时成功支持了动态更新和变长键/范围查询。

Conclusion: Diva和Diva++填补了现有范围过滤器的空白，首次在单一结构中同时实现了理论性能保证、变长支持及动态更新能力，显著提升了键值存储等场景下的查询效率。

Abstract: Range filters are compact probabilistic data structures that answer approximate range emptiness queries. They are used in many domains, e.g., in key-value stores, to quickly rule out the existence of keys in a given query range and avoid searching for them in storage. However, all existing range filters exhibit at least one of three shortcomings: (1) they do not provide any false positive rate or performance guarantees, (2) they do not support variable-length keys and query ranges, and (3) they do not allow dynamic updates.
  We introduce Diva, the first range filter to address all the above challenges simultaneously. Diva learns the dataset's distribution by sampling keys and storing them in a cache-efficient trie. It compresses keys in-between samples by removing their longest common prefix and truncating their suffixes while leaving enough bits in the middle (i.e., an infix) to differentiate the keys in sorted order. It stores infixes in constant-time dynamic data blocks, which it splits to handle insertions and expansions. It processes a range query by traversing the trie and checking for the inclusion of infixes in the target query range.
  We mathematically prove that Diva provides the best possible trade-off between memory and false positive rate on many common real-world data distributions. We extend these benefits to a wider range of real-world workloads by introducing Diva++, an enhanced Diva variant. Diva++ saves memory by removing redundancies among infixes using order-preserving entropy encoding. It then removes any remaining identical infixes and uses the freed space to store more bits of the original keys within compact binary tries.
  We compare Diva and Diva++ to all prior range filters, and show that they achieve a false positive rate on par with the state of the art on real-world datasets while supporting dynamicity and variable-length queries and keys.

</details>


### [305] [The Power of Local Marginals: An $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$-Aspect-Ratio Reduction for Dynamic Weighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27805)
*Jiale Chen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的归约方法，将动态最大权匹配问题中多项式长宽比的实例转化为 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 长宽比的实例，显著优于现有技术在近似值维护和显式匹配维护方面的局部长宽比界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态最大权匹配（MWM）问题中，现有归约方法（如 Gupta 和 Peng 2013，以及 Bernstein 等人 2025）在处理不同权重类别时所需的局部长宽比（aspect ratio）过大，导致算法效率受限的问题。

Method: 基于局部边际（local marginals）的结构性质，将边按权重分类，证明某一类相对于所有低权重类的全局边际贡献，可近似为其在局部权重窗口（长宽比为 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$）内的边际贡献；进而推导出的值组合引理（value composition lemma）和匹配组合引理（matching composition lemma）仅依赖局部窗口的近似最优值。

Result: 1. 在维护 $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-近似最优值时，将局部长宽比从 $\varepsilon^{-Θ(\varepsilon^{-1})}$ 改进为 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$；2. 在维护显式 $(1-\varepsilon)$-近似匹配时，将局部长宽比从 $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 改进为 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$；该归约适用于一般图且兼容部分动态更新。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部边际的结构特性，本文实现了更高效的归约，大幅降低了动态最大权匹配算法中对局部权重窗口长宽比的要求，为设计更优的动态匹配算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study dynamic maximum weight matching (MWM) under edge insertions and deletions in two settings: maintaining a $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-approximation to the optimum weight, and maintaining an explicit $(1-\varepsilon)$-approximate matching. Our main result is a reduction that transforms instances of polynomial aspect ratio into instances of aspect ratio $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$. The reduction applies to general graphs in both settings and is compatible with partially dynamic updates.
  The reduction is based on a structural property of local marginals. After grouping edges into weight classes, the global marginal contribution of one class relative to all lower classes is approximated by its marginal contribution within a local weight window of aspect ratio $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$. Summing these local marginals yields a value composition lemma that uses only approximate optimum values of the local windows. This improves the value reduction of Gupta and Peng (FOCS 2013), whose local aspect ratio is $\varepsilon^{-Θ(\varepsilon^{-1})}$. The same structural property yields an improved matching composition lemma for explicit matchings, reducing the local aspect ratio of Bernstein--Chen--Dudeja--Langley--Sidford--Tu (SODA 2025) from $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ to $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$.

</details>


### [306] [Online Differentially Private Consistent Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27896)
*Edith Cohen,Vadym Doroshenko,Badih Ghazi,Pritish Kamath,Alexander Knop,Ravi Kumar,Ethan Leeman,Pasin Manurangsi,Adam Sealfon,Marika Swanberg*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的差分隐私（DP）归约方法，将在线流式聚类输入转换为私有半核心集（semi-coreset），使得任何非隐私在线聚类算法经后处理即可在保护隐私的同时获得良好的效用，且在近似比、空间和时间复杂度上优于或媲美现有算法，并首次继承了非隐私算法的一致性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线流式设置下差分隐私 $k$-means 和 $k$-median 聚类问题，现有 DP 算法在近似比、空间效率及运行时间上存在局限，且缺乏非隐私算法所具备的一致性（consistency）等优良属性。

Method: 提出一种通用归约框架，将敏感的输入流转化为私有流（即输入流的半核心集），随后将任意非隐私在线聚类算法作为后处理步骤应用于该私有流，从而实现对原始聚类目标的优化。

Result: 该算法在近似比、空间占用和运行时间上匹配或改进了现有最先进算法（如 Epasto et al., 2026; Dupré la Tour et al., 2024），并成功继承了底层非隐私算法的一致性属性，这是此前 DP 算法未能实现的。

Conclusion: 所提出的归约方法不仅提升了在线 DP 聚类的效率与效用，还通过保留非隐私算法的关键特性（如一致性），为构建更鲁棒、高效的隐私保护流式聚类系统提供了通用且强大的理论基础。

Abstract: We study differentially private (DP) $k$-means and $k$-median clustering in the online streaming setting. In this model, points arrive sequentially, and at each time step, we need to output a set of $k$ centers that optimizes the clustering objective for all points seen so far. We give a generic reduction that transforms the (sensitive) input stream into a private stream, which is a semi-coreset of the input stream. This implies that any (non-private) online clustering algorithm, run as a post-processing step, can achieve good utility for the original clustering objective. Our algorithm matches or improves upon the approximation ratio, space usage, and running time of existing algorithms [Epasto et al., 2026, Dupré la Tour et al., 2024]. A key aspect of our reduction is that it inherits desirable properties of the underlying non-private clustering algorithm, such as consistency [Lattanzi and Vassilvitskii, 2017]--a property not satisfied by previous DP algorithms.

</details>


### [307] [A Simpler Analysis of the Bansal-Jiang Quasi Monte-Carlo Algorithm via Haar Wavelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27986)
*Jiaheng Cheng,Agastya Vibhuti Jha,Haotian Jiang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Haar-Besov半范数的等价刻画，简化了Bansal-Jiang随机准蒙特卡洛方法的分析，避免了复杂的傅里叶分解和抵消论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值积分是科学与工程的核心任务，蒙特卡洛和准蒙特卡洛方法各有优缺点。Bansal和Jiang提出的随机QMC方法虽然结合了两者优势并改进了误差界，但其分析过程复杂，需要精细利用二进分解结构和随机性。

Method: 通过Haar分解对平滑变化量σ_SO(f)进行等价刻画，将其与经典的Haar-Besov半范数联系起来，从而提供更概念化、更直接的分析方法。

Result: 建立了σ_SO(f)与Haar-Besov半范数的等价关系，简化了Bansal-Jiang QMC方法的分析，无需使用Hlawka-Zaremba公式、傅里叶分解及复杂的抵消论证。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机QMC方法提供了更简洁的理论框架，将新的平滑变化量概念与经典分析工具联系起来，降低了方法分析的复杂度。

Abstract: Numerical integration---approximating the integral of a function $f$ using $n$ point evaluations---is a central task in science and engineering. The two main paradigms for this problem, the Monte Carlo and quasi-Monte Carlo methods, have distinct strengths and limitations, and a fundamental question is to design a method that combines the benefits of both. \smallskip Building on recent algorithmic advances in discrepancy theory, Bansal and Jiang \cite{BJ25a} gave a randomized QMC method that naturally bridges the MC and QMC error guarantees. Their method also achieves a surprising improvement over the classical Koksma--Hlawka inequality for QMC methods: it attains an error bound of $\widetilde{O}(σ_{\mathsf{SO}}(f)/n)$, where $σ_{\mathsf{SO}}(f)$ is a new notion of \emph{smoothed-out variation} that they introduced and showed to be substantially smaller than the Hardy--Krause variation governing the classical bound. \smallskip However, the analysis in \cite{BJ25a} is quite involved: it must carefully exploit the structure of the dyadic decomposition and the randomness of the algorithm inside a sufficiently fine discretization of the Hlawka--Zaremba formula to obtain cancellations among the high-frequency components in the Fourier decomposition of $f$. The contribution of this article is twofold:
  (1) We give an equivalent characterization of $σ_{\mathsf{SO}}(f)$ in terms of the Haar--Besov seminorm of $f$, relating this new notion of smoothed-out variation to classical quantities.
  (2) Through this characterization, we provide a conceptually simpler and more direct analysis of the Bansal--Jiang QMC method via Haar decomposition, bypassing the use of the Hlawka--Zaremba formula, Fourier decomposition, and the delicate cancellation arguments of \cite{BJ25a} that heavily exploit the structure of dyadic decomposition.

</details>


### [308] [Beyond the Bethe Approximation of the Permanent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28031)
*Nima Anari*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性多项式时间算法，将非负矩阵永久子的近似因子从 $(\sqrt{2})^n$ 改进为 $c^n$（其中 $c<\sqrt{2}$），证明了规范 Bethe 近似并非确定性近似的理论障碍，且该证明过程结合了人类专家与 AI 模型（ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro 和 Codex）的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范 Bethe 近似虽然提供了非负矩阵永久子的确定性近似，但其误差因子 $(\sqrt{2})^n$ 随维度指数增长。研究旨在突破这一指数底数的限制，探索是否存在更优的确定性近似算法，以消除 Bethe 保证作为理论瓶颈的潜在可能性。

Method: 在 Bethe 下界的基础上，引入一种新的“证书”（certificate）机制。该证书专门针对那些导致 Bethe 下界失效（即损失几乎全部因子）的特定矩阵进行优化，从而收紧近似界限。此外，研究采用了人机协作模式：作者提供高层攻击计划，与 ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro 交互开发证明，并利用 Codex 辅助验证证明、组装手稿及排版。

Result: 成功构造出一个绝对常数 $c < \sqrt{2}$，实现了对任意非负矩阵永久子的 $c^n$-近似确定性多项式时间算法。这一结果在理论上优于原有的 $(\sqrt{2})^n$ 界限。

Conclusion: 规范 Bethe 保证并不是非负矩阵永久子确定性近似的不可逾越的障碍。通过引入针对性的新证书，可以显著降低近似因子的指数底数。同时，该研究也展示了大型语言模型在辅助复杂数学证明推导、验证及论文撰写方面的有效性。

Abstract: The canonical Bethe approximation gives a deterministic approximation to the permanent of every nonnegative matrix within a factor of $(\sqrt{2})^n$. We improve the base of this exponential factor: for some absolute constant $c<\sqrt{2}$, there is a deterministic polynomial-time $c^n$-approximation for the permanent of every nonnegative matrix. This shows that the canonical Bethe guarantee is not a barrier for deterministic approximation of the permanent. The proof augments the Bethe lower bound with a new certificate tailored to matrices on which that lower bound loses nearly the full factor.
  The author supplied the high-level plan of attack, and the proof was developed in an interaction with ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro. The author subsequently verified the results. Codex assisted with proof checking, manuscript assembly, and typesetting.

</details>


### [309] [A Configuration-LP Framework for Connected $k$-Median Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28081)
*Kushagra Chatterjee,Rojin Rezvan,Ali Vakilian*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究带连通性约束的重叠k-中位聚类问题，提出基于配置LP的框架，在分配版本上获得O(log²n)近似，在一般版本上以O(k log n)个中心实现O(log²n)成本近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-中位聚类问题缺乏对簇连通性的约束，而实际应用中常需簇在给定图上保持连通。由于度量空间与连通图独立，该问题比标准聚类更具挑战性，且已知分配版本存在Ω(log n)的近似下界，需设计更有效的近似算法。

Method: 提出基于配置线性规划（configuration-LP）的框架，结合覆盖LP技术与根最小密度预言机（rooted minimum-density oracle），分别处理分配版本与一般版本问题。

Result: 分配版本获得O(log²n)近似算法；一般版本采用双标准框架，开放O(k log n)个中心，实现O(log²n)的成本近似比。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了配置LP、覆盖LP与根密度预言机的有效结合，为处理聚类问题中的图论约束提供了新的LP基方法，拓展了连通性约束聚类的近似算法设计思路。

Abstract: We study the \emph{connected $k$-median} clustering problem, a clustering problem that augments the classical $k$-median objective with connectivity constraints. We focus on the \emph{overlapping} variant of the problem, where clusters are allowed to share vertices. In addition to a metric space $(V,d)$, the input contains a connected graph $G$ on the same vertex set $V$ of size $n$. The goal is to select at most $k$ centers $C$ and assign vertices to them so as to minimize the $k$-median cost (i.e., $\sum_{v\in V} d(v,C)$), subject to the constraint that each cluster induces a connected subgraph of $G$. Since the metric space and the connectivity graph are independent, the problem is significantly more challenging than standard clustering. Eube et al.~\cite{eube2025esa} showed that even the assignment version is $Ω(\log n)$-hard to approximate and gave approximation algorithms with guarantees depending polynomially on $k$.
  We develop a configuration-LP-based framework that combines covering LP techniques with a rooted minimum-density oracle. For the assignment version, we obtain an $O(\log^2 n)$-approximation. For the general version, we develop a bicriteria framework that opens $O(k\log n)$ centers while achieving an $O(\log^2 n)$-approximation in cost. %Our results provide a different LP-based approach for handling connectivity constraints in clustering problems and demonstrate that configuration LPs, covering LPs, and rooted density oracles can be combined effectively to obtain approximation guarantees for clustering objectives under graph-theoretic constraints.

</details>


### [310] [A Tight Analysis of Khatri-Rao Oblivious Subspace Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28094)
*Lorenzo Beretta,Cameron Musco*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意固定阶数 $d$ 的 Khatri-Rao 随机素描矩阵，实现 $(1\pm \epsilon)$ 误差的 $k$ 维子空间嵌入仅需 $\tilde O(k/\epsilon^2)$ 的素描维度，填补了其与无结构矩阵之间的理论差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khatri-Rao 素描矩阵广泛应用于具有张量结构数据的随机线性代数算法中，但现有理论无法完全解释其实际性能。特别是，其关键的非感知子空间嵌入（oblivious subspace embedding）性质的理论界限（如 $d=2$ 时为 $O(k^{3/2}/\epsilon^2)$）落后于标准无结构矩阵的紧界 $O(k/\epsilon^2)$。

Method: 利用 Khatri-Rao 素描分布的两个基本性质进行证明：1) 矩阵列是独立且各向同性的；2) 每一列满足弱 Johnson-Lindenstrauss 型矩性质。该方法不依赖复杂推导，仅基于这两个基础特性。

Result: 研究结果表明，对于任意固定阶数 $d$，使用 Khatri-Rao 素描矩阵进行子空间嵌入时，素描维度 $m = \tilde O(k/\epsilon^2)$ 即已足够。

Conclusion: 该工作成功缩小了 Khatri-Rao 矩阵与标准无结构亚高斯素描矩阵在子空间嵌入维度依赖上的理论差距，确立了前者在理论上同样具备最优的维度缩放效率。

Abstract: We study random sketching matrices with Khatri-Rao structure. In particular, we consider the Khatri-Rao product (i.e., column-wise tensor product) $A_1\odot\cdots\odot A_d \in \mathbb R^{(n_1 \cdots n_d) \times m}$ of random matrices $A_i \in \mathbb R^{n_i \times m}$ whose columns are isotropic, independent and sub-Gaussian (e.g., Gaussian matrices). Khatri-Rao sketching matrices are widely applied in randomized algorithms for linear algebraic computation and data analysis, when the input data has tensor structure that allows for fast multiplication with $A_1\odot\cdots\odot A_d$. However, existing theory is not able to fully explain their performance in practice. In particular, despite significant attention, our best bounds for the important \emph{oblivious subspace embedding} property with Khatri-Rao matrices lag behind what is achievable with standard unstructured matrices.
  For embedding a $k$-dimensional subspace to $(1\pm ε)$ error, Bujanović et al. \cite{bujanovic2025subspace} prove that sketching dimension $m = O(k^{3/2}/ε^2)$ suffices in the special case of $d = 2$. Their dependence on $k$ is weaker than the tight bound of $O(k/ε^2)$ known for unstructured sub-Gaussian sketching matrices. In this work, we close this gap, showing that $m = \tilde O(k/ε^2)$ suffices for subspace embedding with a Khatri-Rao sketching matrix with any fixed order $d$. Our proof is simple, leveraging just two basic properties of the Khatri-Rao sketching distribution: 1) the columns of $A_1\odot\cdots\odot A_d \in \mathbb R^{(n_1 \cdots n_d) \times m}$ are independent and isotropic, and 2) each column of $A_1\odot\cdots\odot A_d \in \mathbb R^{(n_1 \cdots n_d) \times m}$ satisfies a weak Johnson-Lindenstrauss type moment property.

</details>


### [311] [Multi-tier Flexible Graph Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28313)
*Karthekeyan Chandrasekaran,Raymond Jiang,Krishna Kalathur*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对网络设计中非均匀边故障问题，提出了多层灵活图连通性模型（k-tier FGC），并为三种变体设计了近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受网络设计中非均匀边故障的启发，需要一种能够处理不同层级边可靠性要求的连通性模型。

Method: 提出了k层灵活图连通性模型，将边分为嵌套层级并设定层级需求，针对三种变体分别设计了基于LP的对数近似算法、组合近似算法和基于LP的2-近似算法。

Result: 为k-tier FGC设计了LP对数近似算法；为最小基数版本设计了依赖层级需求的组合近似算法；为多图连通性变体设计了LP 2-近似算法。

Conclusion: 该模型有效解决了多层级边故障场景下的最小成本连通性问题，为不同变体提供了有效的近似算法解决方案。

Abstract: Motivated by non-uniform edge failures in network design, we introduce a multi-tier model of flexible graph connectivity. In k-tier Flexible Graph Connectivity (k-tier FGC), the input is an undirected graph G=(V, E) with non-negative edge costs, along with a classification of the edges into nested tiers T_1 subseteq T_2 subseteq ... subseteq T_k = E and non-negative integral tier requirements q_1 <= q_2 <= ... <= q_k. A non-empty proper subset R of vertices is safe if it is safe along one of the tiers, i.e., there exists i in [k] such that |delta(R) cap T_i| >= q_i. The goal is to find a minimum cost subset F subseteq E of edges such that the subgraph (V, F) has no unsafe cuts. The case of k=1 corresponds to the min-cost p-edge-connected spanning subgraph problem which is APX-hard. We design approximation algorithms for every fixed constant k for three variants of k-tier FGC: (i) for k-tier FGC, we design an LP-based logarithmic approximation, (ii) for min-cardinality k-tier FGC, we design a combinatorial approximation whose factor depends only on the tier requirements q_1 and q_k, and (iii) for k-tier Flexible Multi-Graph Connectivity, where we are allowed to use multiple copies of each edge while paying the cost of the edge for each chosen copy of the edge, we design an LP-based 2-approximation.

</details>


### [312] [Tight Bounds for Memory Allocation With and Without Request Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28462)
*Michael A. Bender,Alex Conway,Martín Farach-Colton,Hanna Komlós,William Kuszmaul,Nicole Wein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文发现，在内存分配中引入轻微的请求碎片化（$k$-aggregate request fragmentation），可将最优竞争比从经典的$Θ(\log M)$显著降低至$Θ(\log \log M)$，并证明了该结果对确定性和随机算法均紧确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典内存分配问题的确定性在线算法最优竞争比下界为$Θ(\log M)$（1971年确立），但现实中的分配器似乎通过允许将请求拆分为多个片段（即请求碎片化）绕过了这一限制。本文旨在探究请求碎片化是否从根本上改变了内存分配问题的性质及其具体影响。

Method: 提出$k$-aggregate request fragmentation模型，允许分配器将请求拆分，只要同时存在的最大片段数不超过同时存在的最大请求数的$k$倍。通过构建匹配的上界和下界证明，分析在该模型下（即使$k = 1 + o(1)$）确定性和随机算法的竞争比变化。

Result: 即使采用极轻微的碎片化（$k = 1 + o(1)$），最优竞争比也从经典的$Θ(\log M)$坍缩至$Θ(\log \log M)$。该结果对确定性和随机算法均成立，且上下界匹配，证明了其紧确性。

Conclusion: 请求碎片化从根本上改变了内存分配问题的复杂度，使得原本受限于对数级竞争比的难题得以大幅优化至双对数级，解释了现实分配器绕过经典下界的机制。

Abstract: The classical memory-allocation problem captures the task of placing objects of different sizes in memory, while minimizing the so-called memory high-water mark. It has been known since the early 1970s that the optimal competitive ratio for any deterministic online allocator is $Θ(\log M)$, where $M$ is the volume high-water mark of the underlying request sequence.
  This paper begins with a simple observation: many real-world allocators seem to bypass the 1971 lower bound by adopting a slightly different model for memory allocation. These allocators use what we call $k$-aggregate request fragmentation, meaning that the memory allocator is permitted to break requests into multiple fragments, so long as the all-time maximum number of simultaneous fragments is at most $k$ times the all-time maximum number of simultaneous requests.
  We consider the following basic question: Does request fragmentation fundamentally change the problem of memory allocation, and if so, how? Our results come with several surprises. Among these, we find that even using $k = 1 + o(1)$ request fragmentation, the optimal competitive ratio---which was $Θ(\log M)$ in the classical setting---collapses to $Θ(\log \log M)$. This result is shown to be tight with matching upper and lower bounds, applying to both deterministic and randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [313] [Quadratic Probing Insertions Are $ε^{-(1+o(1))}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28512)
*Yang Hu,William Kuszmaul,Jingxun Liang,Stefan Walzer,Huacheng Yu,Renfei Zhou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了二次探测哈希表在负载因子为 $1 - \varepsilon$ 时的期望插入时间为 $\varepsilon^{-(1 + o(1))}$，解决了该数据结构复杂度分析中长达半个世纪的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次探测自1968年提出以来，虽被广泛使用且猜想其在高负载下的期望插入时间为 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$，但学界一直未能证明任何关于 $\varepsilon^{-1}$ 的函数上界 $f(\varepsilon^{-1})$，这一理论空白亟待填补。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导与概率分析，对二次探测哈希表在高负载因子（$1 - \varepsilon$）下的插入操作期望时间复杂度进行理论证明。

Result: 成功证明了期望插入时间为 $\varepsilon^{-(1 + o(1))}$，确立了该数据结构在 $\varepsilon^{-1}$ 的多项式因子范围内的复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 该研究彻底解决了二次探测哈希表期望插入时间复杂度的长期开放问题，将理论界限精确到了次多项式因子（sub-polynomial factors）的范围内。

Abstract: First proposed in 1968, quadratic probing has stood for more than half a century as one of the simplest and most widely used hash-table designs in computer science. It is conjectured that, at load factor $1 - ε$, the hash table achieves $O(ε^{-1})$ expected insertion time. But even proving a bound of the form $f(ε^{-1})$ for any function $f$ has remained open.
  In this paper, we prove that the expected insertion time is $ε^{-(1 + o(1))}$. This settles the complexity of the data structure up to sub-polynomial factors in $ε^{-1}$.

</details>


### [314] [On two proofs of $d^2$ mixing of weighted Dikin walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28566)
*Yuansi Chen,Yunbum Kook*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了加权Dikin游走用于从多面体上的指数分布和截断半正定（PSD）锥采样的混合时间，提出了基于强自洽性、对称性和混合迹正则性的通用混合界，并通过新的高阶自举条件改进了卡方散度下的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在高维几何结构（如多面体和截断PSD锥）上进行高效采样的问题，特别是为了获得更紧致的混合时间界限，以克服现有方法在收敛速度和理论保证上的不足。

Method: 提出了一种控制高概率区域而非逐点控制Metropolis-Hastings接受概率的新框架；利用强自洽性、$\barν$-对称性和混合迹正则性分析局部度量；引入新的四阶自举条件（fourth-order bootstrap condition）以建立更强的卡方散度保证和逐点接受控制；将该框架应用于Lee-Sidford、Lewis权重、John度量及混合屏障。

Result: 1. 在多面体采样中，应用Lee-Sidford、Lewis权重和John度量获得了$\widetilde O(d^2)$的混合界；2. 在截断PSD锥采样中，应用混合屏障获得了$\widetilde O(d^4)$的混合界；3. 在卡方散度下，针对适当缩放的Lee-Sidford度量，将混合界从之前的$\widetilde O(d^{9/4})$改进为$\widetilde O(d^2)$。

Conclusion: 通过引入高概率区域控制策略和四阶自举条件，显著提升了加权Dikin游走在复杂凸体上采样的理论混合时间界限，特别是在卡方散度度量下实现了更优的收敛速率，为高维采样算法提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the mixing time of weighted Dikin walks for sampling from exponential distributions on polytopes and truncated positive-semidefinite (PSD) cones. Our first result gives a general total-variation mixing bound under strong self-concordance, $\barν$-symmetry, and mixed-trace regularity on the local metric. The key idea is to control the Metropolis--Hastings acceptance probability on a high-probability region rather than at every point. Applying this framework to the Lee--Sidford, Lewis-weight, and John metrics yields an $\widetilde O(d^2)$ mixing bound for sampling from polytopes, while applying it to a hybrid barrier yields an $\widetilde O(d^4)$ mixing bound for sampling from truncated PSD cones. Our second result establishes stronger $χ^2$-divergence guarantees and pointwise acceptance control using a new fourth-order bootstrap condition. For a suitably scaled Lee--Sidford metric, this yields an $\widetilde O(d^2)$ mixing bound in $χ^2$-divergence, improving on the previous $\widetilde O(d^{9/4})$ bound.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [Destroy Me: Automatic Artifact Generation for Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27516)
*Zuzanna Krawczyk-Borysiak,Adam Krawczyk,Mateusz Miller,Gabriela Kaczmarek,Sławomir Pakuło,Małgorzata Sokół,Żaneta Swiderska-Chadaj*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Destroy Me"混合框架，通过结合微调的Stable Diffusion和基于物理的过程建模，合成逼真的病理图像伪影进行数据增强，显著提升了深度学习模型在真实世界不完整数据下的诊断鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的病理学深度学习策略倾向于过滤低质量区域以获取“完美数据”，但这会导致有价值的诊断上下文丢失；模型在真实世界数据的不完美性（如伪影）面前表现脆弱，亟需一种能让模型在不完美的环境中蓬勃发展的新范式。

Method: 提出"Destroy Me"混合框架：1) 利用微调的Stable Diffusion模型，在保持组织形态连续性的前提下，将伪影真实地整合到基础组织结构中；2) 结合基于物理的过程建模，合成六种常见伪影（组织折叠、沉淀物、模糊、拼接错误、灰尘和笔迹标记）；3) 使用Kernel Inception Distance (KID) 和颜色Wasserstein距离评估伪影保真度；4) 在肺腺癌模式分类任务中，使用nnU-Net验证该策略。

Result: 在独立真实世界数据集上的验证显示，经过“破坏”补丁训练的模型性能一致优于基线模型：宏观F1分数相对提高了10.5%，Cohen's Kappa ($κ$) 系数相对增加了15%。

Conclusion: 选择性且基于影响权重的数据增强对于平衡实际鲁棒性与保留细微诊断特征至关重要；工程化模型以适应不完美的环境比单纯过滤低质量数据更为有效。

Abstract: Deep learning's diagnostic utility in pathology is constrained by model vulnerability to real-world data imperfections. While current strategies favor "perfect data" by filtering low-quality regions, which can lead to the loss of valuable diagnostic context, we propose a paradigm shift: engineering models to thrive in imperfect environments using "Destroy Me", a hybrid framework for realistic artifact synthesis and robust data augmentation. Our approach combines Stable Diffusion, fine-tuned to preserve morphological continuity by realistically integrating artifacts with the underlying tissue architecture, with physics-based procedural modeling to synthesize six common artifact types: tissue folds, precipitates, blur, stitching errors, dust, and pen markers. Artifact fidelity is assessed using Kernel Inception Distance (KID) and color Wasserstein distance metrics. Validating this strategy on lung adenocarcinoma pattern classification with an nnU-Net, we confirm that models trained on "destroyed" patches consistently outperform baselines on independent real-world datasets. Specifically, we observed a 10.5% relative improvement in macro F1-score and a 15% relative increase in the Cohen's Kappa ($κ$) coefficient. Crucially, our results demonstrate that selective, impact-weighted augmentation is vital for balancing practical robustness with the preservation of subtle diagnostic features.

</details>


### [316] [CARDINAL Predicts Cardiovascular Risk From Non-contrast Cardiac CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27690)
*Roy Gabriel,Nattakorn Kittisut,Jamshid Hassanpour,Michael Galarnyk,Abanoub Abdelmalak,Marly van Assen,Carlo N. De Cecco,Arshed Quyyumi,Ali Adibi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARDINAL框架，利用深度学习从常规非对比心脏CT中提取紧凑表征，在预测主要不良心血管事件（MACE）方面显著优于传统风险方程、冠脉钙化评分及手工影像生物标志物，尤其在长期预测中表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前心血管风险预测受限于临床数据不完整以及将CT影像简化为少量手工特征导致信息丢失的问题，旨在挖掘非对比心脏CT中未被充分利用的预后信息。

Method: 开发CARDINAL框架（基于嵌套解剖潜在嵌入的深度学习表征学习），在17,659名患者队列中，将其与AHA PCE、AHA PREVENT、冠脉钙化（CAC）、分割衍生CT生物标志物及70特征结构放射组学进行对比，评估其在1、3、5及10年MACE预测中的性能。

Result: 在10年预测 horizon 上，CARDINAL联合模型AUROC达0.866±0.020，AUPRC达0.890±0.015，优于最强基线结构放射组学（AUROC 0.826）；同时获得最高生存一致性指数（C-index 0.753）及高低风险三分位风险比（10.78），展现出优异的风险重分类能力。

Conclusion: 非对比心脏CT蕴含超越传统风险方程、CAC评分及工程化影像生物标志物的丰富预后信息，CARDINAL框架能有效提取这些信息以显著提升长期心血管事件预测精度。

Abstract: Cardiovascular risk prediction remains limited by incomplete clinical data and imaging biomarkers that reduce computed tomography (CT) to a small number of handcrafted features. We developed CARDINAL (Cardiovascular Assessment via Representation learning from Deep Imaging with Nested Anatomical Latent embeddings), a clinically grounded framework that learns compact representations from routine non-contrast cardiac CT for major adverse cardiovascular event (MACE) prediction. In 17,659 patients, CARDINAL was evaluated for 1-, 3-, 5-, and 10-year MACE prediction against American Heart Association (AHA) pooled cohort equations (PCE), AHA predicting risk of cardiovascular disease events (PREVENT), coronary artery calcium (CAC), segmentation-derived CT biomarkers, and 70-feature structural radiomics. Gains were largest at longer horizons. At 10 years, CARDINAL (joint) achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.866 $\pm$ 0.020 and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.890 $\pm$ 0.015, compared with an AUROC of 0.826 $\pm$ 0.023 and an AUPRC of 0.826 $\pm$ 0.022 for structural radiomics, the strongest baseline. CARDINAL also achieved the highest survival concordance index (C-index), 0.753 $\pm$ 0.015, and high-versus-low risk-tertile hazard ratio, 10.78 $\pm$ 3.16, with favorable reclassification and exploratory calibration. These findings suggest that non-contrast cardiac CT contains prognostic information beyond conventional risk equations, CAC scoring, and engineered imaging biomarkers.

</details>


### [317] [Do Medical Vision Models Reason About Anatomy? Probing the Spatial Inductive Biases of Learned Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28092)
*Naren Akash,Neeraja Ramanan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文构建了SPAR-Bench评估基准，发现医学视觉编码器虽然能记住器官的常规位置，但缺乏在特定患者图像中进行结构比较和空间推理的能力，且开放权重MLLMs表现更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉编码器通常仅通过诊断准确率或多模态系统整体表现来评估，难以确定其内部表征是否真正支持CT扫描解读所需的空间定位、关系推理和结构比较能力。

Method: 构建了SPAR-Bench基准，包含8个针对多器官腹部CT的探测任务，分离坐标定位、关系推理和空间查询能力；在5种架构配置和3种医学基础模型上进行测试，包括冻结和微调状态，并对比了池化头与全token集的读取方式。

Result: 切片内比较类探测任务表现接近随机猜测，预训练规模、微调或架构改进均无法缩小差距；域内看似解决的任务在零样本迁移下表现降至随机水平，表明其准确性反映的是对标准解剖结构的记忆而非图像计算；使用池化头读取冻结特征可将关系恢复能力从0.7%提升至67.8%；四个开放权重MLLMs对编码器能回答的问题表现接近随机。

Conclusion: 医学视觉编码器主要携带器官常规位置的地图，但缺乏针对特定患者进行结构比较的机制；当前评估方法可能低估了表征能力，而开放权重MLLMs在空间推理任务上表现更差。

Abstract: Interpreting a CT scan means comparing structures on either side, judging how far apart organs sit, and knowing where each one belongs. Medical vision encoders are evaluated on diagnostic accuracy, or through assembled multimodal systems where a failure is hard to attribute, so it remains unclear whether their representations support any of this. We construct SPAR-Bench, eight probes over multi-organ abdominal CT that separate coordinate localization, relational reasoning, and spatial queries, and apply them to five architectural configurations and three medical foundation models, frozen and finetuned. Probes that ask for a comparison within the slice stay at chance, and neither pretraining scale, finetuning, nor architecture closes the gap. Probes that appear solved in domain fall to chance under zero-shot transfer, indicating that their accuracy reflects recall of canonical anatomy rather than computation over the image. Reading the same frozen features with a pooled head rather than the full set of tokens moves relational recovery from 0.7% to 67.8%, so pooled probing understates what a representation holds. Questions the encoders answer well are answered at chance by four open-weight MLLMs. Our results suggest these encoders carry a map of where organs usually lie, and little of the machinery for comparing structures within a particular patient. Code and data will be available at https://spar-bench.github.io.

</details>


### [318] [CheXtriev: Anatomy-Centered Representation for Case-Based Retrieval of Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28137)
*Naren Akash,Arihanth Tadanki,Jayanthi Sivaswamy*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 CheXtriev 的基于图且感知解剖结构的胸部 X 光检索框架，通过图 Transformer 提取特定解剖区域特征，在检索精度和排名质量上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注全局特征，缺乏对特定解剖区域及空间上下文关系的利用，导致在罕见病变检索上效果不佳。

Method: 采用基于图的 Transformer 架构，结合解剖学先验知识，提取特定解剖区域特征并捕捉解剖位置与病变之间的空间上下文关系。

Result: CheXtriev 在检索准确率上比现有最先进方法提升 18% 至 26%，在排名质量上提升 11% 至 23%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解剖学感知的上下文表示实现了更准确、有效且高效的胸部 X 光检索，尤其适用于罕见病变的检索任务。

Abstract: We present CheXtriev, a graph-based, anatomy-aware framework for chest radiograph retrieval. Unlike prior methods focussed on global features, our method leverages graph transformers to extract informative features from specific anatomical regions. Furthermore, it captures spatial context and the interplay between anatomical location and findings. This contextualization, grounded in evidence-based anatomy, results in a richer anatomy-aware representation and leads to more accurate, effective and efficient retrieval, particularly for less prevalent findings. CheXtriv outperforms state-of-the-art global and local approaches by 18% to 26% in retrieval accuracy and 11% to 23% in ranking quality. The code is available at https://github.com/cvit-mip/chextriev.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [319] [Covering 1024 syndromes with 50 columns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27494)
*Stephen Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个覆盖半径为2的二元线性$[50,40]_2$码，将已知最小长度从51降低到50，并利用该结果通过构造方法生成了更长长度的最优码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在突破自2003年以来由Kaikkonen和Rosendahl确立的$R=2$覆盖半径码的长度下界（51），优化Davydov--Marcugini--Pambianco族码的参数，并降低相关码族的渐近密度。

Method: 构造了一个具有覆盖半径2的二元线性$[50,40]_2$码，该码的生成矩阵允许$(2,0)$-划分为十个块；随后应用$\mathrm{QM}_2^2$构造法将该基础码传播至更大的长度。

Result: 确立了$\ell_2(10,2)\le 50$的新上界；通过传播构造得到了长度分别为815（$r=18$）和1631（$r=20$）的经穷举验证的码；导出了长度为$n=51\cdot 2^{r/2-5}-1$的新码族，其渐近密度为$2601/2048$。

Conclusion: 该研究成功刷新了覆盖半径为2的二元线性码的长度记录，提供了具体的矩阵构造及验证源码，并为相关码族的渐近性能分析提供了新的基准。

Abstract: We exhibit a binary linear $[50,40]_2$ code of covering radius $2$, so $\ell_2(10,2)\le 50$, one column below the Kaikkonen--Rosendahl length $51$ that has stood since 2003 and that still seeds the $R=2$ family of Davydov--Marcugini--Pambianco (arXiv:2511.02542). The new matrix admits a $(2,0)$-partition into ten blocks, so Construction $\mathrm{QM}_2^2$ propagates it to exhaustively verified codes of lengths $815$ and $1631$ at $r=18$ and $r=20$, and to the family $n=51\cdot 2^{r/2-5}-1$ of asymptotic density $2601/2048$. The matrices, verifiers, and source are at https://github.com/wustep/maths, pin problems/covering/share/2026-08-24/ at commit 736a38f.

</details>


### [320] [Selective Interference Suppression of Siamese-Net in Heterogeneous Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27635)
*Arkadeep Sinha,Shubham Paul,R. Manivasakan,Nambi Seshadri,R. David Koilpillai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种端到端学习的短块码，用于具有异构成对干扰强度的N用户实高斯干扰信道，同时保持每个接收器的单用户解码。重点研究了存在少数主导干扰者的情况，探讨Siamese式耦合训练是否能自适应地编码和解码，以确保正交性和编码增益之间的最佳权衡。通过4用户不等干扰配置，展示了选择性干扰抑制现象，即学习到的码本主要对主导对接近正交，而弱耦合对保留编码增益所需的对齐。使用潜在空间跨用户相似性统计量化了这种行为，并将其与观察到的BLER鲁棒性联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在存在少数主导干扰者的情况下，Siamese式耦合训练是否能自适应地编码和解码，以确保正交性和编码增益之间的最佳权衡，而不是强制不必要的全球正交性。

Method: 通过4用户不等干扰配置（一个主导对和一对弱背景耦合），使用SiameseNet进行端到端学习，量化潜在空间跨用户相似性统计（最坏情况相干度量、平均相似性度量等），并连接这些几何特征到观察到的BLER鲁棒性。

Result: 学习到的码本主要对主导对接近正交，而弱耦合对保留编码增益所需的对齐，SiameseNet选择性抑制来自各种干扰用户对的干扰，以产生编码增益和由正交性决定的BLER之间的最佳权衡。

Conclusion: SiameseNet能够选择性抑制干扰，优化编码增益和BLER之间的权衡，适应不同的干扰强度配置。

Abstract: We study an end-to-end learnt short-block codes for a $N$-user real Gaussian interference channel with heterogeneous pairwise interference strengths, while keeping single-user decoding at every receiver. In this paper, we study the case wherein only a few dominant interferers exist and investigate whether Siamese-style coupled training can adapt selectively to encode (\& decode) to ensure optimal performance corresponding to best tradeoff between orthogonality and coding gain or it enforces unnecessary global orthogonality oblivious of the reality. Our work focuses on a 4-user unequal-interference configuration with one dominant pair $(1,2)$ (of strength $Λ$) and a weak pair (with weak background coupling ($λ$)), through which we demonstrate a selective interference suppression phenomenon where the learned codebooks become near-orthogonal primarily for the dominant pair, while weakly coupled pairs retain alignment needed for coding gain. We quantify this behaviour using latent-space cross-user similarity statistics (worst-case coherence measure, average similarity measure, etc) and connect these geometric signatures to the observed BLER robustness under unequal interference. It seems that the SiameseNet selectively suppresses the interferences from various interferring user pairs to yield optimal tradeoff between coding gain and BLER dictated by orthogonality.

</details>


### [321] [Low-Altitude Fluid Antenna Network with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27909)
*Tong Zhang,Yanfei Su,Shuai Wang,Wanli Ni,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）和电磁数字孪生（EM-DT）的低空流体天线（FA）网络快速重配置框架，通过联合优化FA位置和波束成形，系统总速率相比固定位置基线提升118.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络（LAWNs）面临动态空-地和空-空信道、突发阻塞和异构干扰等挑战，阻碍了为无人机和电动垂直起降飞行器提供普适通信、感知和定位服务的目标实现。

Method: 提出电磁数字孪生辅助的多智能体强化学习框架，并引入两阶段迁移学习框架以填补仿真到现实的差距，实现流体天线位置和波束成形的联合优化。

Result: 案例研究表明，相比固定位置基线，联合优化FA位置和波束成形可将系统总速率提升118.5%，得益于毫秒级FA阵列动态重配置和对移动空中用户的自适应波束转向。

Conclusion: 流体天线技术通过重配置天线位置解锁额外空间自由度，结合MARL和迁移学习可有效实现低空FA网络的高性能快速重配置，推动低空FA网络的实际应用。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) integrate terrestrial and aerial platforms to provide ubiquitous communication, sensing, and localization services for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. However, dynamic air-ground and air-air channels, abrupt blockages, and heterogeneous interference hinder the realization of this goal. Nevertheless, fluid antenna (FA), a cutting-edge multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technique, overcomes these challenges by reconfiguring antenna positions to unlock additional spatial degrees-of-freedom. In this paper, towards bringing low-altitude FA networks into reality, we study the fast and high-performance FA reconfiguration for low-altitude FA networks with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Specifically, we present an electromagnetic digital twin (EM-DT)-assisted MARL framework. To fill the sim-to-real gap, we introduce a two-stage transfer learning framework. Our case study shows that joint FA positions and beamforming optimization can enhance the system sum-rate by 118.5%, compared to the fixed position baseline. This gain comes from the dynamic millisecond timescale reconfiguration of FA arrays and the adaptive steering of beams toward aerial users with mobility.

</details>
